## Narration 501

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1541)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم خَطَبَ النَّاسَ فِي بَعْضِ مَغَازِيهِ - قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ - فَأَقْبَلْتُ نَحْوَهُ فَانْصَرَفَ قَبْلَ أَنْ أَبْلُغَهُ فَسَأَلْتُ مَاذَا قَالَ فَقِيلَ لِي نَهَى أَنْ يُنْبَذَ فِي الدُّبَّاءِ وَالْمُزَفَّتِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1541]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1541" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, addressed the people in one of his raids. Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;I went towards him, but he finished before I reached him. I asked about what he had said. Someone said to me, &#x27;He forbade preparing nabidh in a gourd or in a jug smeared with pitch</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1541]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1541" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने किसी ग़ज़वे में खुत्बा दिया. मैं भी आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की तरफ सुनने के लिए चला (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) फारिग हो गए. मैंने लोगों से पुछा कि आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने क्या फ़रमाया? लोगों ने कहा: आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने नबीज़ (ड्रिंक) को तुबने (वुडेन वेसल) और मर्तबान में भिगोने से मना फ़रमाया है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1541]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1541" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے کسی غزوے میں خطبہ پڑھا میں بھی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کی طرف چلا سننے کے واسطے لیکن میرے پہنچنے سے پہلے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم فارغ ہو گئے میں نے لوگوں سے پوچھا کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے کیا فرمایا لوگوں نے کہا منع کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے نبیذ بھگونے سے توبنے اور مرتبان میں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1541]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1541" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne kisi ghazwe mein khutba diya. Main bhi aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki taraf sunne ke liye chala, lekin mere pahunchne se pehle aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) farigh ho gaye. Maine logon se poocha ke aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne kya farmaya? Logon ne kaha: Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne nabeez (drink) ko tubne (wooden vessel) aur martabaan mein bhigone se mana farmaya hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1541]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1541" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 502

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ الْعَلاَءِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ يَعْقُوبَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى أَنْ يُنْبَذَ فِي الدُّبَّاءِ وَالْمُزَفَّتِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1542]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1542" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 503

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1543)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، ‏.‏ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى أَنْ يُنْبَذَ الْبُسْرُ وَالرُّطَبُ جَمِيعًا وَالتَّمْرُ وَالزَّبِيبُ جَمِيعًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1543]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1543" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1543</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1543 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from Ata ibn Yasar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade preparing nabidh from nearly ripe dates and fresh dates together, and from dates and raisins together</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1543]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1543" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अत बिन यसर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मना फ़रमाया के गुदर खजूर और पक्की खजूर मिला कर भिगोई जाएँ अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने खजूर और अंगूर मिला कर नबीज़ पीने से और गुदर और पुख़्ता खजूर मिला कर नबीज़ पीने से मना फ़रमाया. इमाम मलिक ने कहा कि हमारे शहर के उलमा का इस बात पर इत्तेफाक है के ये मकरूह है क्यूंकि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इस से मना फ़रमाया है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1543]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1543" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عطا بن یسار رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے منع فرمایا کہ گدر کھجور اور پکی کھجور ملا کر بھگوئی جائیں یا کھجور اور انگور ملا کر بھگوئے جائیں ۔ ابو قتادہ انصاری رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ منع کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے کھجور اور انگور کے ملا کر نیبذ پینے سے اور گدر اور پختہ کھجور کو ملا کر نبیذ پینے سے۔ کہا مالک نے اس امر پر اتفاق کیا ہے ہمارے شہر کے علماء نے کہ یہ مکروہ ہے کیونکہ منع کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اس سے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1543]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1543" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ata bin Yasar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mana farmaya ke gudar khajoor aur pakki khajoor mila kar bhigoi jayen, ya khajoor aur angoor mila kar bhigoye jayen. Abu Qatada Ansari (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne khajoor aur angoor mila kar nabeez peene se aur gudar aur pukhta khajoor mila kar nabeez peene se mana farmaya. Imam Malik ne kaha ke hamare shehar ke ulama ka is baat par ittefaq hai ke ye makrooh hai kyunke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne is se mana farmaya hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1543]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1543" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 504

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1544)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ الثِّقَةِ، عِنْدَهُ عَنْ بُكَيْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الأَشَجِّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحُبَابِ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي قَتَادَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى أَنْ يُشْرَبَ التَّمْرُ وَالزَّبِيبُ جَمِيعًا وَالزَّهْوُ وَالرُّطَبُ جَمِيعًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1544]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1544" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1544</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1544 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from a reliable source from Bukayr ibn Abdullah ibn al-Ashajj from Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Hubab al-Ansari from Abu Qatada al-Ansari that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade making nabidh from dates and raisins together, and nearly ripe dates and fresh dates together. Malik said, &quot;That is the way of doing things among us in which the people of knowledge in our city continue. It is disapproved of because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1544]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1544" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1544</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1544 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझसे मालिक से, उन्होंने एक सिकाह रावी से, उन्होंने बुकैर बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन अशज्ज से, उन्होंने अब्दुर-रहमान बिन अल-हुबाब अल-अन्सारी से, उन्होंने अबू क़तादा अल-अन्सारी से रिवायत किया के रसूलअल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने खजूर और किशमिश को मिला कर नबीज़ बनाने से, और कच्ची खजूरों और पकी खजूरों को मिला कर नबीज़ बनाने से मना फ़रमाया। मालिक ने कहा: हमारे शहर में अहल-ए-इल्म का यही तरीक़ा चला आ रहा है और यह ना-पसंदीदा है क्योंकि रसूलअल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इस से मना फ़रमाया है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1544]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1544" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے مالک سے، انہوں نے ایک ثقہ راوی سے، انہوں نے بکیر بن عبداللہ بن اشج سے، انہوں نے عبدالرحمن بن الحباب الانصاری سے، انہوں نے ابو قتادہ الانصاری سے روایت کیا کہ رسول اللہ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے کھجور اور کشمش کو ملا کر نبیذ بنانے سے، اور کچی کھجوروں اور پکی کھجوروں کو ملا کر نبیذ بنانے سے منع فرمایا۔ مالک نے کہا: ہمارے شہر میں اہلِ علم کا یہی طریقہ چلا آ رہا ہے اور یہ ناپسندیدہ ہے کیونکہ رسول اللہ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے اس سے منع فرمایا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1544]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1544" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhse Malik se, unhone ek siqah raavi se, unhone Bukayr bin Abdullah bin Ashajj se, unhone Abdur-Rahman bin al-Hubab al-Ansari se, unhone Abu Qatada al-Ansari se riwayat kiya ke RasoolAllah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne khajoor aur kishmish ko mila kar nabeed banane se, aur kachi khajooron aur paki khajooron ko mila kar nabeed banane se mana farmaya. Malik ne kaha: Hamare shehar mein ahl-e-ilm ka yahi tareeqa chala aa raha hai aur yeh na-pasandeeda hai kyunke RasoolAllah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne is se mana farmaya hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1544]</p>

## Narration 505

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1545)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ سُئِلَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنِ الْبِتْعِ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ كُلُّ شَرَابٍ أَسْكَرَ فَهُوَ حَرَامٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1545]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1545" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1545</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1545 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from ibn Shihab from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked about mead, and he replied, &#x27;Every drink which intoxicates is haram</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1545]</p>

## Narration 506

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1546)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سُئِلَ عَنِ الْغُبَيْرَاءِ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ خَيْرَ فِيهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَنَهَى عَنْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَسَأَلْتُ زَيْدَ بْنَ أَسْلَمَ مَا الْغُبَيْرَاءُ فَقَالَ هِيَ الأُسْكَرْكَةُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1546]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1546" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1546</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1546 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from Ata ibn Yasar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked about al-Ghubayra. He said, &quot;There is no good in it,&quot; and forbade it. Malik said, &quot;I asked Zayd ibn Aslam, &#x27;What is al- Ghubayra?&#x27; He said, &#x27;It is an intoxicant</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1546]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1546" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अता बिन यासर से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से जवार (मिलेट) की शराब के बारे में सवाल हुआ, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ये बेहतर नहीं है और इस से मना फ़रमाया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1546]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1546" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 507

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1547)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ شَرِبَ الْخَمْرَ فِي الدُّنْيَا ثُمَّ لَمْ يَتُبْ مِنْهَا حُرِمَهَا فِي الآخِرَةِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1547]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1547" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1547</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1547 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said &quot;Whoever drinks wine in this world and does not turn from it in tawba, it is haram for him in the Next World</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1547]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1547" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1547</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1547 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जो शख़्स दुनिया में शराब पिएगा और फिर उस से तौबा नहीं करेगा, तो वो आख़िरत में शराब (के रिज़क़) से महरूम रहेगा।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1547]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1547" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو شخص دنیا میں شراب پئے گا پھر اس سے توبہ نہ کرے گا تو آخرت میں شراب سے محروم رہے گا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1547]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1547" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 508

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1548)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 42</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنِ ابْنِ وَعْلَةَ الْمِصْرِيِّ، ‏.‏ أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ عَمَّا يُعْصَرُ مِنَ الْعِنَبِ فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ أَهْدَى رَجُلٌ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَاوِيَةَ خَمْرٍ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَمَا عَلِمْتَ أَنَّ اللَّهَ حَرَّمَهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ لاَ ‏.‏ فَسَارَّهُ رَجُلٌ إِلَى جَنْبِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ بِمَ سَارَرْتَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَمَرْتُهُ أَنْ يَبِيعَهَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ إِنَّ الَّذِي حَرَّمَ شُرْبَهَا حَرَّمَ بَيْعَهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَفَتَحَ الرَّجُلُ الْمَزَادَتَيْنِ حَتَّى ذَهَبَ مَا فِيهِمَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1548]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1548" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that Ibn Wala al-Misri asked Abdullah ibn Abbas about what is squeezed from the grapes. Ibn Abbas replied, &quot;A man gave the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, a small water-skin of wine. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &#x27;Don&#x27;t you know that Allah has made it haram?&#x27; He said, &#x27;No.&#x27; Then a man at his side whispered to him. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, asked what he had whispered, and the man replied, &#x27;I told him to sell it.&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The One who made drinking it haram has made selling it haram.&#x27; The man then opened the water- skins and poured out what was in them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1548]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1548" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">एक शख़्स रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के लिए शराब की एक मश्क (लेदर बैग) तोहफ़े में लाया। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: क्या तू नहीं जानता के अल्लाह तआला ने इसे हराम किया है? वो बोला: मुझे ख़बर नहीं थी। एक शख़्स ने चुपके से उसके कान में कुछ कहा, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: तुमने क्या कहा? उसने कहा: मैंने इसे बेच डालने को कहा है। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जिसने इसका पीना हराम किया, उसी ने इसका बेचना भी हराम किया है। ये सुन कर उस शख़्स ने मश्क का मुँह खोल दिया और सारी शराब बह गई।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1548]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1548" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ایک شخص رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے واسطے ایک مشک شراب کی تحفہ لایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا کیا تو نہیں جانتا کہ اللہ تعالیٰ نے اس کو حرام کیا ہے وہ بولا مجھے خبر نہیں، ایک شخص نے چپکے سے اس کے کان میں کچھ کہا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے پوچھا تو نے کیا کہا؟ وہ بولا میں نے بیچ ڈالنے کو کہا، آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا جس نے اس کا پینا حرام کیا اس نے اس کا بیچنا بھی حرام کیا یہ سن کر اس شخص نے مشک کا منہ کھول دیا سب شراب بہہ گئی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1548]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1548" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ek shakhs Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke liye sharab ki ek mashk (leather bag) tohfe mein laya. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kya tu nahi janta ke Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne isse haram kiya hai? Wo bola: Mujhe khabar nahi thi. Ek shakhs ne chupke se uske kaan mein kuch kaha, toh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne poocha: Tumne kya kaha? Usne kaha: Maine ise bech dalne ko kaha hai. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jisne iska peena haram kiya, usi ne iska bechna bhi haram kiya hai. Ye sun kar us shakhs ne mashk ka munh khol diya aur saari sharab beh gayi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1548]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1548" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 509

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1560)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ، يَقُولُ مَضَتِ السُّنَّةُ أَنَّ الرَّجُلَ، إِذَا أَصَابَ امْرَأَتَهُ بِجُرْحٍ أَنَّ عَلَيْهِ عَقْلَ ذَلِكَ الْجُرْحِ وَلاَ يُقَادُ مِنْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا ذَلِكَ فِي الْخَطَإِ أَنْ يَضْرِبَ الرَّجُلُ امْرَأَتَهُ فَيُصِيبَهَا مِنْ ضَرْبِهِ مَا لَمْ يَتَعَمَّدْ كَمَا يَضْرِبُهَا بِسَوْطٍ فَيَفْقَأُ عَيْنَهَا وَنَحْوَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمَرْأَةِ يَكُونُ لَهَا زَوْجٌ وَوَلَدٌ مِنْ غَيْرِ عَصَبَتِهَا وَلاَ قَوْمِهَا فَلَيْسَ عَلَى زَوْجِهَا إِذَا كَانَ مِنْ قَبِيلَةٍ أُخْرَى مِنْ عَقْلِ جِنَايَتِهَا شَىْءٌ وَلاَ عَلَى وَلَدِهَا إِذَا كَانُوا مِنْ غَيْرِ قَوْمِهَا وَلاَ عَلَى إِخْوَتِهَا مِنْ أُمِّهَا إِذَا كَانُوا مِنْ غَيْرِ عَصَبَتِهَا وَلاَ قَوْمِهَا فَهَؤُلاَءِ أَحَقُّ بِمِيرَاثِهَا وَالْعَصَبَةُ عَلَيْهِمُ الْعَقْلُ مُنْذُ زَمَانِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى الْيَوْمِ وَكَذَلِكَ مَوَالِي الْمَرْأَةِ مِيرَاثُهُمْ لِوَلَدِ الْمَرْأَةِ وَإِنْ كَانُوا مِنْ غَيْرِ قَبِيلَتِهَا وَعَقْلُ جِنَايَةِ الْمَوَالِي عَلَى قَبِيلَتِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1560]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1560" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he heard Ibn Shihab say, &quot;The precedent of the sunna when a man injures a woman is that he must pay the blood- money for that injury and there is no retaliation against him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;That is an accidental injury, when a man strikes a woman and hits with a blow what he did not intend, for instance, if he struck her with a whip and cut her eye open and the like of that.&quot; Malik said about a woman who has a husband and children who are not from her paternal relatives or her people, that since he is from another tribe, there is no blood-money against her husband for her criminal action, nor any against her children if they are not from her people, nor any against her maternal brothers when they are not from her paternal relations or her people. These are entitled to her inheritance but only the paternal relations have paid blood-money from since the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Until today it is like that with the mawla of a woman. The inheritance they leave goes to the children of the woman even if they are not from her tribe, but the blood-money of the criminal act of the mawla is only against her tribe</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1560]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1560" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शिहाब कहते थे के ये सुन्नत चली आती है के मर्द अपनी औरत को अगर ज़ख़्मी करे तो उस से दियत ली जायेगी और क़सास ना लिया जायेगा। मालिक ने कहा ये जब है के मर्द खता से अपनी औरत को ज़ख़्मी करे, अमदन ये काम ना करे (अगर अमदन करे तो क़सास वाजिब होगा)। मालिक ने कहा जिस औरत का ख़ावन्द या लड़का उसकी क़ौम से ना हो तो औरत की जिनायत की दियत में वो शरीक ना होगा, इसी तरह उसका लड़का या अख्याफ़ी भाई जब और क़ौम से हों क्योंकि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के वक़्त से दियत कुनबे वालों पर होती है, मगर मीरास लड़के और अख्याफ़ी भाइयों को मिलेगी जैसे औरत के मवाली (ग़ुलामान-ए-आज़ाद) की मीरास उसके लड़के को मिलेगी, अगरचे उसकी क़ौम से ना हो, मगर उसकी जिनायत की दियत औरत के कुनबे वालों पर होगी।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1560]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1560" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب کہتے تھے کہ یہ سنت چلی آتی ہے کہ مرد اپنی عورت کو اگر زخمی کرے تو اس سے دیت لی جائے گی اور قصاص نہ لیا جائے گا ۔ کہا مالک نے یہ جب ہے کہ مردخطا سے اپنی عورت کو زخمی کرے عمداً یہ کام نہ کرے (اگر عمداً کرے تو قصاص واجب ہوگا)۔ کہا مالک نے جس عورت کا خاوند یا لڑکا اس کی قوم سے نہ ہو تو عورت کی جنایت کی دیت میں وہ شریک نہ ہوگا اسی طرح اس کا لڑکا یا اخیافی بھائی جب اور قوم سے ہوں کیونکہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے وقت سے دیت کنبے والوں پر ہوتی ہے مگر میراث لڑکے اور اخیافی بھائیوں کو ملے گی جیسے عورت کے موالی (غلامان آزاد) کی میراث اس کے لڑکے کو ملے گی اگرچہ اس کی قوم سے نہ ہو مگر اس کی جنایت کی دیت عورت کے کنبے والوں پر ہو گی۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1560]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1560" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Shihab kehte the ke ye sunnat chali aati hai ke mard apni aurat ko agar zakhmi kare to us se diyat li jayegi aur qisas na liya jayega. Malik ne kaha ye jab hai ke mard khata se apni aurat ko zakhmi kare, amdan ye kaam na kare (agar amdan kare to qisas wajib hoga). Malik ne kaha jis aurat ka khawand ya ladka uski qaum se na ho to aurat ki jinayat ki diyat mein wo shareek na hoga, isi tarah uska ladka ya akhyafi bhai jab aur qaum se hon kyunke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke waqt se diyat kunbe walon par hoti hai, magar meeras ladke aur akhyafi bhaiyon ko milegi jaise aurat ke mawali (ghulaman-e-azad) ki meeras uske ladke ko milegi, agarche uski qaum se na ho, magar uski jinayat ki diyat aurat ke kunbe walon par hogi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1560]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1560" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 510

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1562)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى فِي الْجَنِينَ يُقْتَلُ فِي بَطْنِ أُمِّهِ بِغُرَّةٍ عَبْدٍ أَوْ وَلِيدَةٍ فَقَالَ الَّذِي قُضِيَ عَلَيْهِ كَيْفَ أَغْرَمُ مَا لاَ شَرِبَ وَلاَ أَكَلْ وَلاَ نَطَقَ وَلاَ اسْتَهَلّ وَمِثْلُ ذَلِكَ بَطَلْ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا هَذَا مِنْ إِخْوَانِ الْكُهَّانِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1562]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1562" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave a judgement that the compensation for a foetus killed in its mother&#x27;s womb was a slave or slave-girl of fair complexion and excellence. The one against whom the judgement was given said, &quot;Why should I pay damages for that which did not drink or eat or speak or make any cry. The like of that is nothing.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;This is only one of the brothers of the diviners.&quot; He disapproved of the rhyming speech of the man&#x27;s declaration</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1562]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1562" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1562</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1562 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यिब से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पेट के बच्चे के क़त्ल पर एक ग़ुलाम या लौंडी देने का हुक्म दिया। जिस शख़्स पर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने दियत का हुक्म दिया, उसने कहा मैं कैसे तव्वा दूँ उस बच्चे का जिसने ना पिया, ना खाया, ना बोला और ना रोया, ऐसे शख़्स का ख़ून हदर (ला-ग़्व) है और उस में कोई दियत नहीं। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया ये शख़्स काहिनों (fortune tellers) का भाई है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1562]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1562" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے حکم کیا پیٹ کے بچے میں جو قتل کیا جائے ایک غلام یا لونڈی دینے کا جس پر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے دیت دینے کا حکم کیا وہ بولا کیونکر میں تاوان دوں اس بچے کا جس نے نہ پیا نہ کھایا نہ بولا نہ رویا ایسے شخص کا خون ہدر ہے یعنی لغو ہے اس میں دیت نہیں، رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا یہ شخص کاہنوں کا بھائی ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1562]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1562" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pet ke bache ke qatl par ek ghulam ya laundi dene ka hukm diya. Jis shakhs par aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne diyat ka hukm diya, usne kaha main kaise tawwan dun us bache ka jisne na piya, na khaya, na bola aur na roya, aise shakhs ka khoon hadar (la-ghw) hai aur usmein koi diyat nahi. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ye shakhs kahinon (fortune tellers) ka bhai hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1562]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1562" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 511

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1567)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، يَذْكُرُ أَنَّ الْمُوضِحَةَ، فِي الْوَجْهِ مِثْلُ الْمُوضِحَةِ فِي الرَّأْسِ إِلاَّ أَنْ تَعِيبَ الْوَجْهَ فَيُزَادُ فِي عَقْلِهَا مَا بَيْنَهَا وَبَيْنَ عَقْلِ نِصْفِ الْمُوضِحَةِ فِي الرَّأْسِ فَيَكُونُ فِيهَا خَمْسَةٌ وَسَبْعُونَ دِينَارًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ فِي الْمُنَقَّلَةِ خَمْسَ عَشْرَةَ فَرِيضَةً ‏.‏ قَالَ وَالْمُنَقَّلَةُ الَّتِي يَطِيرُ فِرَاشُهَا مِنَ الْعَظْمِ وَلاَ تَخْرِقُ إِلَى الدِّمَاغِ وَهِيَ تَكُونُ فِي الرَّأْسِ وَفِي الْوَجْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الْمَأْمُومَةَ وَالْجَائِفَةَ لَيْسَ فِيهِمَا قَوَدٌ ‏.‏ وَقَدْ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ لَيْسَ فِي الْمَأْمُومَةِ قَوَدٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْمَأْمُومَةُ مَا خَرَقَ الْعَظْمَ إِلَى الدِّمَاغِ وَلاَ تَكُونُ الْمَأْمُومَةُ إِلاَّ فِي الرَّأْسِ وَمَا يَصِلُ إِلَى الدِّمَاغِ إِذَا خَرَقَ الْعَظْمَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ لَيْسَ فِيمَا دُونَ الْمُوضِحَةِ مِنَ الشِّجَاجِ عَقْلٌ حَتَّى تَبْلُغَ الْمُوضِحَةَ وَإِنَّمَا الْعَقْلُ فِي الْمُوضِحَةِ فَمَا فَوْقَهَا وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم انْتَهَى إِلَى الْمُوضِحَةِ فِي كِتَابِهِ لِعَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ فَجَعَلَ فِيهَا خَمْسًا مِنَ الإِبِلِ وَلَمْ تَقْضِ الأَئِمَّةُ فِي الْقَدِيمِ وَلاَ فِي الْحَدِيثِ فِيمَا دُونَ الْمُوضِحَةِ بِعَقْلٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1567]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1567" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said heard Sulayman ibn Yasar mention that a face wound in which the bone was bared was like a head wound in which the bone was bared, unless the face was scarred by the wound. Then the blood-money is increased by one half of the blood-money of the head wound in which the skin was bared so that seventy five dinars are payable for it. Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that the head wound with splinters has fifteen camels.&quot; He explained, &quot;The head wound with splinters is that from which pieces of bone fly off and which does not reach the brain. It can be in the head or the face.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things in our community, is that there is no retaliation for a wound to the brain or a belly wound, and Ibn Shihab has said, &#x27;There is no retaliation for a wound to the brain.&#x27; &quot; Malik explained, &quot;The wound to the brain is what pierces the bones to the brain. This type of wound only occurs in the head. It is that which reaches the brain when the bones are pierced.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that there is no blood-money paid on any head wound less than one which lays bare the skull. Blood-money is payable only for the head wound that bares the bone and what is worse than that. That is because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, stopped at the head wound which bared the bone in his letter to Amr ibn Hazm. He made it five camels. The imams, past and present, have not made any blood- money payable for injuries less than the head wound which bares the bone</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1567]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझसे मालिक से रिवायत किया के याहया बिन सईद ने सुलईमान बिन यसर को ज़िक्र करते सुना के चेहरे का वह ज़ख्म जिस में हड्डी खुल जाये दागदार हो जाये. तब दियत में सर के उस ज़ख्म की दियत का निस्फ़ इज़ाफ़ा किया जाता है जिस में जिल्द खुल जाये चुनांचा इस के लिए पचत्तर दीनार ऐडा किये जायेंगे. मलिक ने कहा: हमारी जमात में जो तरीक़ा है वह यह है के सर के चोट वाले ज़ख्म में पन्द्रह ऊँट हैं. उन्होंने वज़ाहत की: सर का चोट वाला ज़ख्म वह है जिस से हड्डी के टुकड़े उड़ जाएं और जो दिमाग़ तक न पहुंचे. यह सिर या चेहरे पर हो सकता है. मलिक ने कहा: हमारी जमात में मुत्तफ़िक़ा तरीक़ा यह है के दिमाग़ के ज़ख्म या पेट के ज़ख्म का कोई क़िसास नहीं है के ज़ख्म का कोई क़िसास नहीं है.&#x27; मलिक ने वज़ाहत की: दिमाग़ का ज़ख्म वह है जो हड्डियों को चीर कर दिमाग़ तक पहुँच जाये. यह ज़ख्म सिर्फ सर में होता है. मलिक ने कहा: हमारी जमात में अमल यह है के सर के उस ज़ख्म पर कोई दियत नहीं जो खोपड़ी को न खोले. दियत सिर्फ उस ज़ख्म पर है जो हड्डी को खोल दे. इस की वजह यह है के रसूलल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अम्र बिन हज़्म के नाम अपने खत में हड्डी खोलने वाले ज़ख्म पर पांच ऊँट मुकर्रर किये थे. एम्मा ने हड्डी खोलने वाले ज़ख्म से कम चोटों पर कोई दियत मुकर्रर नहीं की है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1567]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1567" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے مالک سے روایت کیا کہ یحییٰ بن سعید نے سلیمان بن یسار کو ذکر کرتے سنا کہ چہرے کا وہ زخم جس میں ہڈی کھل جائے، سر کے اس زخم کی طرح ہے جس میں ہڈی کھل جائے، سوائے اس کے کہ چہرہ زخم سے داغدار ہو جائے۔ تب دیت میں سر کے اس زخم کی دیت کا نصف اضافہ کیا جاتا ہے جس میں جلد کھل جائے، چنانچہ اس کے لیے پچھتر دینار ادا کیے جائیں گے۔ مالک نے کہا: ہماری جماعت میں جو طریقہ ہے وہ یہ ہے کہ سر کے چوٹ والے زخم میں پندرہ اونٹ ہیں۔ انہوں نے وضاحت کی: سر کا چوٹ والا زخم وہ ہے جس سے ہڈی کے ٹکڑے اڑ جائیں اور جو دماغ تک نہ پہنچے۔ یہ سر یا چہرے پر ہو سکتا ہے۔ مالک نے کہا: ہماری جماعت میں متفقہ طریقہ یہ ہے کہ دماغ کے زخم یا پیٹ کے زخم کا کوئی قصاص نہیں ہے، اور ابن شہاب نے کہا ہے، &#x27;دماغ کے زخم کا کوئی قصاص نہیں ہے۔&#x27; مالک نے وضاحت کی: دماغ کا زخم وہ ہے جو ہڈیوں کو چیر کر دماغ تک پہنچ جائے۔ یہ زخم صرف سر میں ہوتا ہے۔ مالک نے کہا: ہماری جماعت میں عمل یہ ہے کہ سر کے اس زخم پر کوئی دیت نہیں جو کھوپڑی کو نہ کھولے۔ دیت صرف اس زخم پر ہے جو ہڈی کو کھول دے۔ اس کی وجہ یہ ہے کہ رسول اللہ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے عمرو بن حزم کے نام اپنے خط میں ہڈی کھولنے والے زخم پر پانچ اونٹ مقرر کیے تھے۔ ائمہ نے ہڈی کھولنے والے زخم سے کم چوٹوں پر کوئی دیت مقرر نہیں کی ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1567]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1567" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhse Malik se riwayat kiya ke Yahya bin Saeed ne Sulayman bin Yasar ko zikr karte suna ke chehray ka woh zakhm jis mein haddi khul jaye, sar ke us zakhm ki tarah hai jis mein haddi khul jaye, siwaye is ke ke chehra zakhm se daaghdaar ho jaye. Tab diyat mein sar ke us zakhm ki diyat ka nisf izafa kiya jata hai jis mein jild khul jaye, chunancha is ke liye pachattar dinar ada kiye jayenge. Malik ne kaha: Hamari jamaat mein jo tareeqa hai woh yeh hai ke sar ke chot wale zakhm mein pandrah oont hain. Unhone wazahat ki: Sar ka chot wala zakhm woh hai jis se haddi ke tukray ud jayein aur jo dimagh tak na pahunche. Yeh sar ya chehray par ho sakta hai. Malik ne kaha: Hamari jamaat mein muttafiqa tareeqa yeh hai ke dimagh ke zakhm ya pait ke zakhm ka koi qisas nahi hai, aur Ibn Shihab ne kaha hai, &#x27;Dimagh ke zakhm ka koi qisas nahi hai.&#x27; Malik ne wazahat ki: Dimagh ka zakhm woh hai jo haddiyon ko cheer kar dimagh tak pahunch jaye. Yeh zakhm sirf sar mein hota hai. Malik ne kaha: Hamari jamaat mein amal yeh hai ke sar ke us zakhm par koi diyat nahi jo khopdi ko na khole. Diyat sirf us zakhm par hai jo haddi ko khol de. Is ki wajah yeh hai ke RasoolAllah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Amr bin Hazm ke naam apne khat mein haddi kholne wale zakhm par paanch oont muqarrar kiye thay. Aimma ne haddi kholne wale zakhm se kam choton par koi diyat muqarrar nahi ki hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1567]</p>

## Narration 512

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1576)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يُسَوِّي بَيْنَ الأَسْنَانِ فِي الْعَقْلِ وَلاَ يُفَضِّلُ بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ مُقَدَّمَ الْفَمِ وَالأَضْرَاسِ وَالأَنْيَابِ عَقْلُهَا سَوَاءٌ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ فِي السِّنِّ خَمْسٌ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَالضِّرْسُ سِنٌّ مِنَ الأَسْنَانِ لاَ يَفْضُلُ بَعْضُهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1576" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that his father made all the teeth the same in the blood-money and did not prefer any kind over others. Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that the front teeth, molars, and eye-teeth have the same blood-money. That is because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The tooth has five camels.&#x27; The molar is one of the teeth and he did not prefer any kind over the others</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1576" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उरवाह बिन ज़ुबैर कहते थे के अगले ज़माने में सब दाँतों की दियत बराबर थी, कोई दूसरे पर ज़्यादा ना थी। कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक यह हुकुम है के दाँत और कुचलियाँ और दाढ़ें सब बराबर हैं, क्योंकि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने हर दाँत में पाँच ऊँट का हुकुम किया, दाढ़ भी एक दाँत है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1576" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر کہتے تھے کہ اگلے زمانے میں سب دانتوں کی دیت برابر تھی کوئی دوسرے پر زیادہ نہ تھی ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم ہے کہ دانت اور کچلیاں اور داڑھیں سب برابر ہیں کیونکہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ہر دانت میں پانچ اونٹ کا حکم کیا داڑھ بھی ایک دانت ہے۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Zubair kehte the ke agle zamane mein sab daanton ki diyat barabar thi, koi dusre par zyada na thi. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek yeh hukum hai ke daant aur kuchliyan aur daarhein sab barabar hain, kyunke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne har daant mein paanch oont ka hukum kiya, daarh bhi ek daant hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

## Narration 513

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1586)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُعَيْبٍ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنْ بَنِي مُدْلِجٍ - يُقَالُ لَهُ قَتَادَةُ - حَذَفَ ابْنَهُ بِالسَّيْفِ فَأَصَابَ سَاقَهُ فَنُزِيَ فِي جُرْحِهِ فَمَاتَ فَقَدِمَ سُرَاقَةُ بْنُ جُعْشُمٍ عَلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ اعْدُدْ عَلَى مَاءِ قُدَيْدٍ عِشْرِينَ وَمِائَةَ بَعِيرٍ حَتَّى أَقْدَمَ عَلَيْكَ فَلَمَّا قَدِمَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَخَذَ مِنْ تِلْكَ الإِبِلِ ثَلاَثِينَ حِقَّةً وَثَلاَثِينَ جَذَعَةً وَأَرْبَعِينَ خَلِفَةً ثُمَّ قَالَ أَيْنَ أَخُو الْمَقْتُولِ قَالَ هَا أَنَا ذَا ‏.‏ قَالَ خُذْهَا فَإِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لَيْسَ لِقَاتِلٍ شَىْءٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Amr ibn Shuayb that a man of the Banu Mudlij called Qatada threw a sword at his son and it struck his thigh. The wound bled profusely and he died. Suraqa ibn Jusham came to Umar ibn al-Khattab and mentioned that to him Umar said to him, &quot;At the watering place of Qudayd count one hundred and twenty camels and wait until I come to you.&quot; When Umar ibn al-Khattab came to him, he took thirty four-year-old camels, thirty five-year-old camels, and forty pregnant camels from them. Then he said, &quot;Where is the brother of the slain man?&quot; He said, &quot;Here.&quot; He said, &quot;Take them. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The killer gets nothing</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अम्र बिन शुऐब से रिवायत है के बनी मुदलज में से एक शख़्स जिसका नाम क़तादा था, उसने अपने लड़के को तलवार मारी जो उसकी पिंडली में लगी, ख़ून बंद न हुआ और आख़िर मर गया। फिर सुराक़ा बिन जुशम हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आए और उन से बयान किया। हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा क़दीद के पास 120 ऊंट तैयार रख जब तक मैं वहां आऊं। जब हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) वहां आए तो उन ऊंटों में से तीन हिक्क़े, बीस जज़ाए और चालीस हिक्क़े लिए। फिर कहा कौन है मक़तूल का भाई? उसने कहा क्यों, मैं मौजूद हूं न! कहा तो ये सब ऊंट ले ले, इस वास्ते के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया क़ातिल को मीरास नहीं मिलती। सईद बिन मुसय्यब और सुलईमान बिन यासार से सवाल हुआ के माह-ए-हराम में अगर कोई क़त्ल करे तो दियत में सख़्ती करेंगे? उन्होंने कहा नहीं, बल्कि बढ़ा देंगे बज़ह इन महीनों की हुरमत के। फिर सईद से पूछा गया के अगर कोई ज़ख़्मी करे इन महीनों में तो उसकी भी दियत बढ़ा देंगे जैसे क़त्ल की दियत बढ़ा देंगे? सईद ने कहा हां।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عمرو بن شعیب سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے بنی مدلج میں سے جس کا نام قتادہ تھا اپنے لڑکے کو تلوار ماری وہ اس کے پنڈلی میں لگی خون بند نہ ہوا آخر مر گیا تو سراقہ بن جعشم حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس آئے اور ان سے بیان کیا حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا قدید کے پاس ایک سو بیس اونٹ تیار رکھ جب تک میں وہاں آؤں جب حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ وہاں آئے تو ان اونٹوں میں سے تین حقے اور بیس جزعے لئے اور چالیس حقے لئے پھر کہا کون ہے مقتول کا بھائی؟ اس نے کہا کیوں میں موجود ہوں نا! کہا تو یہ سب اونٹ لے لے اس واسطے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا قاتل کو میراث نہیں ملتی سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار سے سوال ہوا کہ ماہ حرام میں اگر کوئی قتل کرے تو دیت میں سختی کریں گے انہوں نے کہا نہیں بلکہ بڑھا دیں گے بوجہ ان مہینوں کی حرمت کے پھر سعید سے پوچھا کہ اگر کوئی زخمی کرے ان مہینوں میں تو اس کی بھی دیت بڑھا دیں گے جیسسے قتل کی دیت بڑھا دیں گے سعید نے کہا ہاں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Amr bin Shuaib se riwayat hai ke Bani Mudlij mein se ek shakhs jiska naam Qatada tha, usne apne ladke ko talwar maari jo uski pindli mein lagi, khoon band na hua aur aakhir mar gaya. Phir Suraqa bin Jusham Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaye aur un se bayan kiya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha Qadeed ke paas 120 oont taiyar rakh jab tak main wahan aaun. Jab Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) wahan aaye to un oonton mein se teen hiqqe, bees jaza&#x27;e aur chalees hiqqe liye. Phir kaha kaun hai maqtool ka bhai? Usne kaha kyun, main mojood hoon na! Kaha to ye sab oont le le, is waaste ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya qatil ko miras nahi milti. Saeed bin Musayyib aur Sulaiman bin Yasar se sawal hua ke mah-e-haram mein agar koi qatl kare to diyat mein sakhti karenge? Unhone kaha nahi, balkeh badha denge bajah in mahino ki hurmat ke. Phir Saeed se poocha gaya ke agar koi zakhmi kare in mahino mein to uski bhi diyat badha denge jaise qatl ki diyat badha denge? Saeed ne kaha haan.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

## Narration 514

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1589)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَأَبِي، سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ جَرْحُ الْعَجْمَاءِ جُبَارٌ وَالْبِئْرُ جُبَارٌ وَالْمَعْدِنُ جُبَارٌ وَفِي الرِّكَازِ الْخُمُسُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1589" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1589</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1589 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab and Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The wound of an animal is of no account and no compensation is due for it. The well is of no account and no compensation is due for it. The mine is of no account and no compensation is due for it and a fifth is due for buried treasures.&quot; (Al-kanz:see Book 17). Malik said, &quot;Everyone leading an animal by the halter, driving it, and riding it is responsible for what the animal strikes unless the animal kicks out without anything being done to it to make it kick out. Umar ibn al-Khattab imposed the blood-money on a person who was exercising his horse.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is more fitting that a person leading an animal by the halter, driving it, or riding it incur a loss than a person who is exercising his horse.&quot; (See hadith 4 of this book). Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community about a person who digs a well on a road or ties up an animal or does the like of that on a road used by muslims, is that since what he has done is included in that which he is not permitted to do in such a place, he is liable for whatever injury or other thing arises from that action. The blood-money of that which is less than a third of the full blood- money is owed from his own personal property. Whatever reaches a third or more, is owed by his tribe. Any such things that he does which he is permitted to do on the muslims&#x27; road are something for which he has no liability or loss. Part of that is a hole which a man digs to collect rain, and the beast from which the man alights for some need and leaves standing on the road. There is no penalty against anyone for this.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who went down a well, and another man followed behind him, and the lower one pulled the higher one and they fell into the well and both died He said, &quot;The tribe of the one who pulled him in is responsible for the blood-money.&quot; Malik spoke about a child whom a man ordered to go down into a well or to climb a palm tree and he died as a result. He said, &quot;The one who ordered him is liable for whatever befalls him, be it death or something else.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute is that women and children are not obliged to pay blood-money together with the tribe in the blood-moneys which the tribe must pay. The blood-money is only obligatory for a man who has reached puberty.&quot; Malik said that the tribe could bind themselves to the blood-money of mawali if they wished. If they refused, they were people of the diwan or were cut off from their people. In the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, people paid the blood-money to each other as well as in the time of Abu Bakr as-Siddiq before there was a diwan. The diwan was in the time of Umar ibn al-Khattab. No one other than one&#x27;s people and the ones holding the wala&#x27; paid blood- money for one because the wala&#x27; was not transferable and because the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The wala&#x27; belongs to the one who sets free.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The wala&#x27; is an established relationship.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community about animals that are injured is that the person who causes the injury pays whatever of their value has been diminished.&quot; Malik said about a man condemned to death and one of the other hudud befell him, &quot;He is not punished for it. That is because the killing overrides all of that, except for slander. The slander remains hanging over the one to whom it was said because it will be said to him, &#x27;Why do you not flog the one who slandered you?&#x27; I think that the condemned man is flogged with the hadd before he is killed, and then he is killed. I do not think that any retaliation is inflicted on him for any injury except killing because killing overrides all of that.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that when a murdered person is found among the main body of a people in a village or other place, the house or place of the nearest people to him is not responsible. That is because the murdered person can be slain and then cast at the door of some people to shame them by it. No one is responsible for the like of that.&quot; Malik said about a group of people who fight with each other and when the fight is broken up, a man is found dead or wounded, and it is not known who did it, &quot;The best of what is heard about that is that there is blood-money for him, and the blood-money is against the people who argued with him. If the injured or slain person is not from either of the two parties, his blood-money is against both of the two parties together</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1589" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मालिक से, उन्होंने इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसैयब और अबू सलमा इब्न अब्द अर-रहमान से और उन्होंने अबू हुरयरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत किया के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जानवर का ज़ख्म रायगां है और उसकी कोई दियत नहीं, कुंआ रायगां है, कान रायगां है और दफीना में पांचवां हिस्सा है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مالک سے، انہوں نے ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے سعید بن مسیب اور ابوسلمہ بن عبدالرحمٰن سے اور انہوں نے ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت کیا کہ رسول اللہ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے فرمایا: جانور کا زخم رائیگاں ہے اور اس کی کوئی دیت نہیں، کنواں رائیگاں ہے، کان رائیگاں ہے اور دفینہ میں پانچواں حصہ ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1589" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne Malik se, unhone Ibn Shihab se, unhone Said ibn al-Musayyab aur Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman se aur unhone Abu Hurayra (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat kiya ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Janwar ka zakhm rayegan hai aur uski koi diyat nahi, kunwa rayegan hai, kaan rayegan hai aur dafeena mein paanchwa hissa hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

## Narration 515

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1596)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 44</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي لَيْلَى بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ سَهْلٍ، عَنْ سَهْلِ بْنِ أَبِي حَثْمَةَ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ رِجَالٌ، مِنْ كُبَرَاءِ قَوْمِهِ أَنَّ عَبْدَ، اللَّهِ بْنَ سَهْلٍ وَمُحَيِّصَةَ خَرَجَا إِلَى خَيْبَرَ مِنْ جَهْدٍ أَصَابَهُمْ فَأُتِيَ مُحَيِّصَةُ فَأُخْبِرَ أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ سَهْلٍ قَدْ قُتِلَ وَطُرِحَ فِي فَقِيرِ بِئْرٍ أَوْ عَيْنٍ فَأَتَى يَهُودَ فَقَالَ أَنْتُمْ وَاللَّهِ قَتَلْتُمُوهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا وَاللَّهِ مَا قَتَلْنَاهُ ‏.‏ فَأَقْبَلَ حَتَّى قَدِمَ عَلَى قَوْمِهِ فَذَكَرَ لَهُمْ ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ أَقْبَلَ هُوَ وَأَخُوهُ حُوَيِّصَةُ وَهُوَ أَكْبَرُ مِنْهُ وَعَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ فَذَهَبَ مُحَيِّصَةُ لِيَتَكَلَّمَ وَهُوَ الَّذِي كَانَ بِخَيْبَرَ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ كَبِّرْ كَبِّرْ ‏&quot;‏ يُرِيدُ السِّنَّ فَتَكَلَّمَ حُوَيِّصَةُ ثُمَّ تَكَلَّمَ مُحَيِّصَةُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ إِمَّا أَنْ يَدُوا صَاحِبَكُمْ وَإِمَّا أَنْ يُؤْذِنُوا بِحَرْبٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِمْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي ذَلِكَ فَكَتَبُوا إِنَّا وَاللَّهِ مَا قَتَلْنَاهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِحُوَيِّصَةَ وَمُحَيِّصَةَ وَعَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ ‏&quot;‏ أَتَحْلِفُونَ وَتَسْتَحِقُّونَ دَمَ صَاحِبِكُمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا لاَ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَفَتَحْلِفُ لَكُمْ يَهُودُ ‏&quot;‏ قَالُوا لَيْسُوا بِمُسْلِمِينَ ‏.‏ فَوَدَاهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنْ عِنْدِهِ فَبَعَثَ إِلَيْهِمْ بِمِائَةِ نَاقَةٍ حَتَّى أُدْخِلَتْ عَلَيْهِمُ الدَّارَ ‏.‏ قَالَ سَهْلٌ لَقَدْ رَكَضَتْنِي مِنْهَا نَاقَةٌ حَمْرَاءُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1596]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1596" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu Layla ibn Abdullah ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Sahl from Sahl ibn Abi Hathma that some of the great men of his people informed him that Abdullah ibn Sahl and Muhayyisa went out to Khaybar because extreme poverty had overtaken them. Muhayyisa returned and said that Abdullah ibn Sahl had been killed and thrown in a shallow well or spring. The jews came and he said, &quot;By Allah! You have killed him.&quot; They said, &quot;By Allah! We have not killed him!&quot; Then he made for his people and mentioned that to them. Then he, his brother Huwayyisa, who was older than him, and Abd ar-Rahman, set out. Muhayyisa began to speak, as he had been at Khaybar. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;The greater first, the greater first,&quot; meaning in age. So Huwayyisa spoke and then Muhayyisa spoke. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Either they pay your companion&#x27;s blood-money or we will declare war against them.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, wrote that to them and they wrote, &quot;By Allah, we did not kill him!&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to Huwayyisa, Muhayyisa, and Abd ar-Rahman, &quot;Do you swear and claim the blood of your companion?&quot; They said, &quot;No.&quot; He said, &quot;Shall the jews swear to you?&quot; They said, &quot;But they are not muslims.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave blood-money from his own property, and sent them one hundred camels to their house. Sahl added, &quot;A red camel among them kicked me</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1596]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1596" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सहल बिन आबि हाशम को खबर दी कुछ लोगों ने जो उसकी क़ौम के मुअज़्ज़ज़ थे कि अब्दुल्लाह बिन सहल और मुहय्यिसा फ़क़्र और फ्लॉस की वजह से खयबेर को गए. मुहय्यिसा के पास एक शख्स आया और बयां किया कि अब्दुल्लाह बिन सहल को किसी ने क़त्ल करके कुएं में या चश्मे में डाल दिया है. मुहय्यिसा यह सुनकर ख़ैबर के यहूदियों के पास आये और कहा क़सम अल्लाह की तुमने इसको क़त्ल किया है. यहूदियों ने कहा क़सम अल्लाह की हमने क़त्ल नहीं किया इसको. फिर मुहय्यिसा अपनी क़ौम के पास आये और उनसे बयां किया बिन सहल (जो अब्दुल्लाह बिन सहल मक़तूल के भाई थे) रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आये. मुहय्यिसा ने चाहा कि मैं बात करूँ क्यूंकि वही खयबेर को गए थे तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया बुज़ुर्गी की रिआयत कर. हुवेईसा ने पहले बयां किया फ़रमाया तो यहूदी तुम्हारे क़त्ल की दियत दें या जंग करें. फिर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने यहूदियों को इस बारे में लिखा. उन्होंने जवाब में लिखा कि क़सम अल्लाह की हमने इसको क़त्ल नहीं किया. तब रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने हुवेईसा और मुहय्यिसा और अब्दुर रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुम) से कहा तुम कसम खाओ कि यहूदियों ने इसको मारा है तो दियत के हकदार हो गए. उन्होंने कहा हम क़सम न खायेंगे. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया आचा अगर यहूदी क़सम खा लें कि हमने नहीं मारा? उन्होंने कहा या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) वह मुसलमान नहीं हैं. तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपने पास से दियत ऐडा की. सहल कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उनके पास सौ ऊँट भेजे उनके घरों पर मारी थी.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1596]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1596" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سہل بن ابی حثمہ کو خبر دی کچھ لوگوں نے جو اس کی قوم کے معزز تھے کہ عبداللہ بن سہل اور محیصہ فقر اور افلاس کی وجہ سے خبیر کو گئے محیصہ کے پاس ایک شخص آیا اور بیان کیا کہ عبداللہ بن سہل کو کسی نے قتل کر کے کنوئیں میں یا چشمے میں ڈال دیا ہے محیصہ یہ سن کر خیبر کے یہودیوں کے پاس آئے اور کہا قسم اللہ کی تم نے اس کو قتل کیا ہے یہودیوں نے کہا قسم اللہ کی ہم نے قتل نہیں کیا اس کو، پھر محیصہ اپنی قوم کے پاس آئے اور ان سے بیان کیا بعد اس کے محیصہ اور ان کے بھائی حویصہ جو محیصہ سے بڑے تھے اور عبدالرحمن بن سہل (جو عبداللہ بن سہل مقتول کے بھائی تھے) رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے پاس آئے محیصہ نے چاہا کہ میں بات کروں کیونکہ وہی خبیر کو گئے تھے تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا بزرگی کی رعایت کر۔ حویصہ نے پہلے بیان کیا پھر محیصہ نے بیان کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا تو یہودی تمہارے قتل کی دیت دیں یا جنگ کریں پھر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے یہودیوں کو اس بارے میں لکھا انہوں نے جواب میں لکھا کہ قسم اللہ کی ہم نے اس کو قتل نہیں کیا تب رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے حویصہ اور محیصہ اور عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کہا تم قسم کھاؤ کہ یہودیوں نے اس کو مارا ہے تو دیت کے حقدار ہو گے انہوں نے کہا ہم قسم نہ کھائیں گے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا اچھا اگر یہودی قسم کھالیں کہ ہم نے نہیں مارا انہوں نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم وہ مسلمان نہیں ہیں تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے اپنے پاس سے دیت ادا کی سہل کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے ان کے پاس سو اونٹ بھیجے ان کے گھروں پر ان میں سے ایک سرخ اونٹنی نے مجھے لات ماری تھی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1596]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1596" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Sahl bin Abi Hashma ko khabar di kuch logon ne jo uski qoum ke muazzaz the ki Abdullah bin Sahl aur Muhayyisa faqr aur iflaas ki wajah se Khyber ko gaye. Muhayyisa ke paas ek shakhs aaya aur bayan kiya ki Abdullah bin Sahl ko kisi ne qatl karke kuyein mein ya chashme mein daal diya hai. Muhayyisa yeh sunkar Khyber ke Yahudiyon ke paas aaye aur kaha qasam Allah ki tumne isko qatl kiya hai. Yahudiyon ne kaha qasam Allah ki humne qatl nahi kiya isko. Phir Muhayyisa apni qoum ke paas aaye aur unse bayan kiya, baad uske Muhayyisa aur unke bhai Huwayyisa jo Muhayyisa se bade the aur Abdur Rahman bin Sahl (jo Abdullah bin Sahl maqtool ke bhai the) Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aaye. Muhayyisa ne chaha ki main baat karun kyunki wahi Khyber ko gaye the to Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya buzurgi ki riayat kar. Huwayyisa ne pehle bayan kiya, phir Muhayyisa ne bayan kiya. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya to Yahudi tumhare qatl ki diyat dein ya jang karein. Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Yahudiyon ko is baare mein likha. Unhone jawab mein likha ki qasam Allah ki humne isko qatl nahi kiya. Tab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Huwayyisa aur Muhayyisa aur Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhum) se kaha tum qasam khao ki Yahudiyon ne isko mara hai to diyat ke haqdar ho ge. Unhone kaha hum qasam na khayenge. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya acha agar Yahudi qasam kha lein ki humne nahi mara? Unhone kaha Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) woh musalman nahi hain. To Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apne paas se diyat ada ki. Sahl kehte hain ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne unke paas sau oont bheje unke gharon par, un mein se ek surkh oontni ne mujhe laat maari thi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1596]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1596" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 516

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1598)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 45</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ يَحْيَى، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ إِسْحَاقَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَهُمْ فِي مِكْيَالِهِمْ وَبَارِكْ لَهُمْ فِي صَاعِهِمْ وَمُدِّهِمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ يَعْنِي أَهْلَ الْمَدِينَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1598]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1598" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 517

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1599)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 45</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كَانَ النَّاسُ إِذَا رَأَوْا أَوَّلَ الثَّمَرِ جَاءُوا بِهِ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَإِذَا أَخَذَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ اللَّهُمَّ بَارِكْ لَنَا فِي ثَمَرِنَا وَبَارِكْ لَنَا فِي مَدِينَتِنَا وَبَارِكْ لَنَا فِي صَاعِنَا وَبَارِكْ لَنَا فِي مُدِّنَا اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّ إِبْرَاهِيمَ عَبْدُكَ وَخَلِيلُكَ وَنَبِيُّكَ وَإِنِّي عَبْدُكَ وَنَبِيُّكَ وَإِنَّهُ دَعَاكَ لِمَكَّةَ وَإِنِّي أَدْعُوكَ لِلْمَدِينَةِ بِمِثْلِ مَا دَعَاكَ بِهِ لِمَكَّةَ وَمِثْلِهِ مَعَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ يَدْعُو أَصْغَرَ وَلِيدٍ يَرَاهُ فَيُعْطِيهِ ذَلِكَ الثَّمَرَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1599]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1599" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1599 ]</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Suhayl ibn Abi Salih from his father that Abu Hurayra said, &quot;When people saw the first fruits of the season, they brought them to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, took them and said, &#x27;O Allah! Bless us in our fruits. Bless us in our city. Bless us in our sa and bless us in our mudd. O Allah! Ibrahim is Your slave, Your Khalil and Your Prophet. I am Your slave and Your Prophet. He prayed to You for Makka. I pray to You for Madina for the like of what He prayed to You for Makka, and the like of it with it.&#x27; Then he called the smallest child he saw and gave him the fruits</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1599]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1599" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1599 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है की जब लोग पहला मेवा देखते तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आते. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) इसको लेकर फ़रमाते बरकत दे हमारे शहर में और बरकत दे हमारे सअ में और बरकत दे हमारे मुद्द में. ऐ परवरदिगार इब्राहिम (अलैहिस्सलाम) ने जो तेरे बन्दे और तेरे दोस्त हैं और तेरे नबी थे नबी हूँ जैसे इब्राहिम ने दुआ की थी मक्का के लिए और उतनी और उसके साथ (इशारा करके).&#x27; फिर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) सब से छोटे बच्चे को जो मौजूद होता बुलाते और वह मेवा उसको दे देते.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1599]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1599" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ جب لوگ پہلا میوہ دیکھتے تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے پاس آتے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم اس کو لے کر فرماتے اے پروردگار برکت دے ہمارے پھلوں میں اور برکت دے ہمارے شہر میں اور برکت دے ہمارے صاع میں اور برکت دے ہمارے مد میں، اے پروردگار ابراہیم نے جو تیرے بندے اور تیرے دوست ہیں اور تیرے نبی تھے دعا کی تھی مکہ کے واسطے تیرا بندہ ہوں اور نبی ہوں جیسے ابراہیم نے دعا کی تھی مکہ کے لئے اور اتنی اور اس کے ساتھ(اشارہ کرکے) پھر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم سب سے چھوٹے بچے کو جو موجود ہوتا بلاتے اور وہ میوہ اس کو دے دیتے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1599]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1599" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ki jab log pehla mewa dekhte to Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aate. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) isko lekar farmate, &#x27;Ae Parwardigar barkat de hamare phalon mein aur barkat de hamare shahar mein aur barkat de hamare saa mein aur barkat de hamare mudd mein. Ae Parwardigar Ibrahim (Alaihissalam) ne jo tere bande aur tere dost hain aur tere Nabi the, dua ki thi Makka ke waste, tera banda hoon aur Nabi hoon jaise Ibrahim ne dua ki thi Makka ke liye aur utni aur uske saath (ishara karke).&#x27; Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) sab se chhote bachche ko jo maujood hota bulate aur woh mewa usko de dete.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1599]</p>

## Narration 518

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1600)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 45</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ قَطَنِ بْنِ وَهْبِ بْنِ عُمَيْرِ بْنِ الأَجْدَعِ، أَنْ يُحَنَّسَ، مَوْلَى الزُّبَيْرِ بْنِ الْعَوَّامِ أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ، كَانَ جَالِسًا عِنْدَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فِي الْفِتْنَةِ فَأَتَتْهُ مَوْلاَةٌ لَهُ تُسَلِّمُ عَلَيْهِ فَقَالَتْ إِنِّي أَرَدْتُ الْخُرُوجَ يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ اشْتَدَّ عَلَيْنَا الزَّمَانُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ اقْعُدِي لُكَعُ فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَصْبِرُ عَلَى لأْوَائِهَا وَشِدَّتِهَا أَحَدٌ إِلاَّ كُنْتُ لَهُ شَفِيعًا أَوْ شَهِيدًا يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1600]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1600" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Qatan ibn Wahb ibn Umayr ibn al-Ajda that Yuhannas, the mawla of az-Zubayr ibn al-Awwam informed him that he was sitting with Abdullah ibn Umar during the troubles (at the time of al-Hajaj ibn Yusuf) . A female mawla of his came and greeted him. She said, &quot;I want to leave, Abu Abd ar-Rahman. The time is harsh for us.&quot; Abdullah ibn Umar said to her, &quot;Sit down, O you with little knowledge, for I have heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &#x27;No one will be patient in hunger and hardship in it (Madina) except that I will be a witness or intercede for him on the Day of Rising</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1600]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1600" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
